The preview archive fixture supplied independently hash-verified original bytes; public production player delivery still needs release verification. Full waves, sustained play, saves and audio remain unverified. Launch is now approved for release.

### Barrack current-runtime retest and callback defect

Fresh original 1.0.4 download again matches the receipt above. At source `4d2c64b90585aed2f08bd193ff4ae6214a769658`, bounded native startup still remains black on 68K. PPC reaches the original trial at frontend tick 1,080; Not Yet dismisses it, but most menu artwork is absent. Explicit 8-bit and default 16-bit menu captures match exactly. No gameplay is qualified. The original FAQ confirms a fat 68K/PPC application.

[Issue #4403](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4403) contains the completed bounded reproduction and targeted register trace: Barrack creates a ten-byte picture header, loads the original full PICT separately, installs a native `getPicProc` descriptor at CQDProcs offset 44 in the current port, then calls DrawPicture. The callback entry is never executed. Small sprite pictures pass full resource data and render. The PPC DrawPicture path currently reads only the supplied handle. A structured guest callback continuation and streamed picture input path are needed; no fix, browser approval or publication is claimed.

Fresh 12 October retest using immutable production-code build
`0f24e29c473c5ed912fa7cca50be45e38698ae11`, including the fixes subsequently
merged in #4622/#4626, reproduces the startup blockers. The original 1.0.4
download again matches the 3,081,805-byte receipt. Explicit 68K/depth 8 stays
black at 600 and 1,080 frontend ticks. Explicit PPC/depth 8 shows the intact
30-day trial at 1,080; Not Yet dismisses it but menu artwork remains largely
absent. Captures inspected; zero assertions and no gameplay qualification.

Fresh production packing (5,147,248 bytes) has 19 records / 18 unique paths:
Documentation ƒ/Icon (CR) occurs twice with different resource payloads. Both
records and their ordering are retained; independent installed inner-fork/Finder
parity, including that overwrite scope, remains pending. Independent unar
extracts the outer SVCT installer, not the installed game files. Do not claim
nineteen distinct original files or completed inner parity. The game has three
CODE resources, one cfrg and a `Joy!peffpwpc` data fork: actual 68K and PPC slices,
one game. Game data is 150,323 bytes, SHA-256
`caa55fecab47568cafeb5f440b2be91a12bae14bc12832c60630680e648dbaf9`;
resource fork is 175,945 bytes, SHA-256
`da97472aca0fdf3d11dd7d676411df3607ee11b955e93b02632558d8da53f5cf`.
Fresh original licence inspection retains complete unchanged nonprofit
redistribution permission and the 30-day trial. Callback support, gameplay,
browser, hosting and launch remain pending in #4403.

## Angband 2.7.8 original archive intake

The [VintageAppleMac A index](https://www.vintageapplemac.com/software/games/a/) supplies two distinct original ports of the same game. [Angband 2.7.8.sit](https://www.vintageapplemac.com/files/games/Angband%202.7.8.sit) is 336,502 bytes, SHA-256 `e37f6ca2500a92c0aa8c65c7dff765a7868e33910d8699fc5aeaae792c03f34d`; thirty files expand, including the resource-only application, documentation and data. [Angband 2.7.8 PPC.sit](https://www.vintageapplemac.com/files/games/Angband%202.7.8%20PPC.sit) is 400,492 bytes, SHA-256 `91ce28a571189fff8401b2614264c1df47b561dc9e52d14f944e1f17c4df9ffe`; thirty-nine files expand, including MacAngband•PPC, port notes, documentation and data. Do not repack or count these as two games.

The bundled lib/help/version.txt retains an affirmative copying and distribution grant for educational, research and nonprofit purposes, conditional on retaining the copyright and statement. Modern GPL permission is not substituted for these historical terms. The PPC port Read Me describes Maarten Hazewinkel’s Macintosh/PowerMac port and its 1.0.6 history, without a replacement redistribution restriction. Archive-wide terms review and gameplay qualification remain pending.

At runtime source `4d2c64b90585aed2f08bd193ff4ae6214a769658`, the 68K application reaches a preferences warning; its normal OK button leads to the Angband main window and an instruction to choose New/Open in File. The separate PPC archive reaches its main window with an initialization message. Both actual captures were inspected. These are startup observations, not character creation, dungeon gameplay, browser approval or publication.

## Swoop live v0.88.0 verification

[Release #4402](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4402) merged as `7b225ccb5ab01b0a7ecaa1d60b902fb21b306390`. Its [whole release run](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/actions/runs/38058014084) succeeded, including website deployment, all six native packages and final checksum upload. [The public route](https://systemless.org/swoop/) serves module `systemless-org-8201efa43bac6130.js`. Fresh ordinary Chrome loads actual hosted archive bytes without request interception or preview fixtures. The normal 68K worker at configured 8 MHz, WebGL, starts an active wave, moves the ship with X and fires a visible yellow shot with period. A clean reload separately verifies the preserved 30-day trial and stationary Not Yet dismissal to the unregistered menu. Public crossOriginIsolated is false; preview isolation is not attributed to this run.

The actual hosted archive again returns HTTP 200, 2,739,051 bytes and the pinned original SHA-256 `53dbe824bc2a21c4b6a6b1e2c510a01d6d700f67d3401f66a1ab8cc4d98a70d5`. Gameplay captures were inspected. Full waves, sustained play, saves and audio remain unverified. This qualifies Swoop as the sixth new live game in this intake, without claiming the baseline launch flags are independently audited.

Angband’s bundled `lib/help/general.txt` additionally contains the maintainer’s explicit game/source distribution permission, subject to the existing notices and other incorporated-code restrictions. Native character creation is reached on both ports. The earlier A-at-sex probe was invalid: exact historical birth.c accepts M/F for sex, then letter choices for race/class. Corrected M → A → A selects Male, Human and Warrior and reaches the stat-rolling prompt on both ports. The completed PPC event trace delivers the expected character and neutral modifiers. No keyboard compatibility defect is established by the invalid probe. [Qualification issue #4405](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4405) records the correction; gameplay remains pending.

## Sigma Chess original 5.1.3 installer intake

The [original shareware installer archive](https://www.vintageapplemac.com/files/games/Sigma%20Chess%205.1.3%20Shareware.sit) is 2,327,631 bytes, SHA-256 `64e9036b520945b23164b4aa7086431e5eb9f4e94b83bbe013092bf3dd9e6619`. Its one outer 68K installer contains an ST65 data fork of 2,184,947 bytes. The [container compatibility issue](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4421) records the missing intake support separately from the guest ADCR resource-hook fault in [#4418](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4418).

The new bounded ST65 parser and existing method-14 decoder extract 244 files without modifying or repacking the original archive. Independent CRC-16 checks match all 768 original file/folder headers and decoded resource/data forks. The main application `∑ Chess 5.1.3 ƒ/∑ Chess 5.1.3` has a 511,147-byte data fork beginning `Joy!peff` with `pwpc` architecture, one PPC application cfrg and no CODE resources. Version resources identify 5.1.3. The 68K outer installer is not a 68K game port.

The installed `Read me first!` release notes, February 18, 2001, describe Lite as time-unlimited freeware and Pro as a paid upgrade. Section 6 permits Lite distribution only in original archive form, prohibits modification and sale, and requires author permission for physical-media inclusion. Pro redistribution is prohibited. All original limitations, registration tools, notices, plug-ins, sample games and libraries remain intact. A scan of all 244 files and both forks finds the matching main-game licence and the registration utility’s unchanged-distribution notice; no conflicting distribution restriction was found. Actual PPC gameplay/browser qualification remain pending; this is not a second distinct game and does not add another catalogue record.


## Moria original Compact Pro container

Target 79 uses the unchanged [Moria 1.1.1.cpt.sit distribution](https://www.vintageapplemac.com/files/games/Moria%201.1.1.cpt.sit): SHA-256 `f835a419625bcc16ef2c91576562d20af62b33d0bb6c7d728139e9b5c2284725`, 290684 bytes. The outer StuffIt contains the original 290471-byte Compact Pro container. Its six files include the game, top-level Read Me, and four Documentation files. Independent extraction confirms a resource-only 68K application with 12 CODE resources and no PPC cfrg; a PPC version is not inferred.

The original application's TEXT 129 notice explicitly permits copying and distribution for educational, research and nonprofit purposes when its copyright statement remains included. The original manual also prohibits sales or for-profit distribution without written permission. The complete archive retains these notices and every original file. This is the historical port's notice, not an inferred modern GPL grant.

[Issue #4429](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4429) records the original no-executable load failure. Normal Compact Pro recognition checks the header, bounded flattened directory and raw CRC before interpreting names. The existing Systemless fork decompressor is shared with Smaller Installer, with bounded expansion, checked fork/header/directory ranges, file CRCs, path/depth and duplicate safeguards. [Primary format reference](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/MacPaw/XADMaster/master/XADCompactProParser.m). No external implementation is copied.

The production decoder extracts all six files with both forks matching independent extraction byte-for-byte. All eight focused native tests pass, including the existing installer regressions, invalid containers, resource-only applications and nested folder paths. The unchanged outer archive now starts in 68K mode and its original File > New opens character creation. Human and Male choices advance through original prompts to the statistics screen. These inspected startup/creation captures contain zero assertions and alone do not qualify gameplay or a catalogue launch. Subsequent asserted town movement is recorded below; browser testing, dungeon movement and catalogue publication remain pending.


## Monkey Shines public release verification

[Public evidence](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4435#issuecomment-6101343692) verifies the actual [Monkey Shines route](https://systemless.org/monkey-shines/) on v0.92.1 / `70f1b124e92f`. The initial v0.92.0 catalogue record omitted the explicit launch flag; [correction #4436](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4436) and [release #4437](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4437) resolve that defect. Production-generated configuration was explicitly checked for `approved: true`, avoiding reliance on an absent/defaulted field.

Fresh Chrome uses the real public object without interception or local substitution. The game's object request returns HTTP 200; an independent fetch in the same browser verifies its complete 3684474-byte response against the pinned original SHA-256. Inspected captures show original trial dismissal, New Game, the locked registered worlds, Spooked first-room entry, ordinary ArrowRight movement and an airborne ArrowUp jump. Both held keys are released. The actual 68K worker uses WebGL and default 25 MHz pacing with two ticks per paint; public `crossOriginIsolated` is false. The initial observation connection stalled, so a separate connection inspects and controls the same running owned browser through ordinary input; the guest is not restarted or modified.

The [complete release run](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/actions/runs/38079208981) passes, including deployment and all six native packages with checksum files. This is the tenth independently qualified new live game. Original PPC [#4434](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4434), room completion, key collection, sustained play, saves, audio and editor use remain unverified.

### Moria bounded native movement follow-through

After File > New, Human/Male selection, original statistics acceptance, Warrior selection and naming, the unchanged Moria archive enters town. Keypad 6, 8 and 4 move the player east, north and west. Inspected frames locate the player glyph at `(424,249)`, then `(429,249)`, `(429,238)` and `(424,238)`. A fresh replay passes seven measured pixel assertions at 4009 frontend ticks / 4584 guest ticks, with zero exhausted budgets. This qualifies bounded native 68K town movement; browser testing, dungeon descent, sustained play, combat, saves and catalogue publication remain pending. The original archive remains unmodified and all six files and notices are preserved.


## Moria catalogue intake and native dungeon qualification

[Moria catalogue issue #4441](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4441) and the [staged entry](catalogue/moria.md) record the complete original Purple X 1.1.1 port. Fresh upstream and CI-intake downloads both match `f835a419625bcc16ef2c91576562d20af62b33d0bb6c7d728139e9b5c2284725` / 290684 bytes. Original main-application Compact Pro timestamps fall in 1994; version resources identify 1.1.1 by Richard Knuckey and UMoria 5.5.2. The metadata year refers to this colour Macintosh port; an exact release day is not inferred.

The [extended native evidence](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4441#issuecomment-6101515779) adds original staircase descent to 50 feet and west/north movement inside the dungeon. A fresh repeat passes all 12 measured assertions at 4967 frontend / 5542 guest ticks, with zero exhausted budgets. The initial east/south attempts meet room walls and are not counted as movement. The unedited catalogue screenshot is an actual native town-movement frame; no game archive or scenario is added to Git.

The staged entry validates alongside 198 merged records, giving 199 locally validated entries. At initial intake launch was disabled pending ordinary browser gameplay, exact asset promotion, launch approval and public release verification. The follow-through below records promotion and browser qualification; public release verification remains pending. No PPC port is inferred from the original 68K-only package. Combat, sustained play, saves and audio remain unverified.

### Moria browser follow-through

The v0.92.2 worker/WebGL preview at source `52271b3080b428a9228e10a260313f37ed72eb07`
reached town through ordinary File > New and character creation. Inspected captures
show west/east movement, descent to 50 feet, then east and south dungeon movement.

CORS workflow reports a Cloudflare authentication error. Preview intake therefore
supplied only the exact independently downloaded R2 archive, after verifying
SHA-256 `f835a419625bcc16ef2c91576562d20af62b33d0bb6c7d728139e9b5c2284725`
and 290684 bytes. No game bytes or guest state were changed. Production-origin
archive requests receive the expected CORS header; actual public-route replay
is still required after release.

Testing exposed the shared Down-arrow preset's keypad-5 mapping, while Moria
uses keypad 2 for south. The catalogue provides its own Down-arrow mapping;
the rebuilt preview confirms normal Left and Down input visibly move the player
west and south.

shareware distribution with Register retained, shareware presentation and only
cost-recovery distribution charges. It names version 1.0.1 despite being bundled
with 1.1.1; version scope and separate artwork-copying restrictions remain under
review. No archive or image is hosted or launch-approved.

Original native 68K v0.93.0 setup reaches Klondike, moves A♦ to its foundation
and drags 4♠ onto 5♥, revealing 2♣. A fresh twelve-assertion replay passes at
1881 frontend / 2460 guest ticks, zero exhausted frames. The original PPC v0.93.0
setup halts at unsupported FillRoundRect after player-name acceptance;
[focused blocker #4460](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4460).
A separate [PICT metadata fix #4461](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4461)
repairs the original registration text on both slices in rebuilt native v0.95.0;
three measured prompt assertions pass on each route. That fix has merged after
all CI gates passed. The fresh v0.95.0 PPC name-acceptance failure is recorded
on [#4460](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4460#issuecomment-6103064729);
[drawing repair #4464](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4464) has
merged after all exact-head CI checks and 118 PPC QuickDraw tests passed.
The original native v0.95.0 with both repairs passes twelve existing 68K legal
card-move assertions at 1881 frontend / 2460 guest ticks, zero exhausted frames.
Its actual PPC setup/board replay now completes at 1881 frontend / 1861 guest
ticks, zero exhausted frames, and Return moves an ace to a foundation, score 0→1.
A fresh eight-assertion actual-PPC ace replay also passes at 1778 frontend /
1758 guest ticks, zero exhausted frames; its captures are inspected again.
The fixed 68K tableau drag is not qualified on the different PPC deal.
Missing PPC instruction text and card suits remain open in
[#4466](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4466).
[Drawing repair native evidence](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4460#issuecomment-6103240824).
These repairs do not establish full PPC game fidelity.
Browser, distribution/screenshot scope, hosting and publication remain pending.

## Galactica 1.1.2 — target 677, original fat package

Inspected 11 October 2026. [Original archive](https://www.vintageapplemac.com/files/games/Galactica%201.1.2.sit): 1,093,837 bytes, SHA-256 `06db3e41350751cf17cd3ea21d27e941213f49354755b2c67f2510de8fa0f03f`. Independent unar and production StuffIt extraction match all fourteen files and twenty-eight forks. The original main executable contains fourteen CODE resources and a `Joy!peffpwpc` data fork with a `pwpc` cfrg fragment: both CPU slices are present.

The bundled English language module's original documentation permits redistribution of unregistered copies with the entire unchanged package, including documentation. Retain the $10 registration terms, Register application, music, all language modules and tutorial. The package is not hosted yet.

Selector `Galactica 1.1.2/Galactica`, depth 8. Original StuffIt 5 metadata contains seventeen entries / fourteen files, with literal slashes inside the six language-module basenames, such as `Galactica/en`. [Archive-component PR #4490](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4490) is merged as `f37139f5f37b595f13eedaf5ca3955ccffcfed76`; [guest basename/FSSpec PR #4491](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4491) is merged as `af265ee7c7bd5bc3f8f6f6aac28acaa455e117e7`. Both passed exact-head CI. Single- and multi-source production browser prepacking produce identical 2,608,454-byte output with all fourteen files / twenty-eight forks unchanged and original component boundaries retained. This validates ingestion, not browser gameplay.

The two archive metadata regressions pass, including literal slashes in file/folder components and same-prefix real folders. The two guest enumeration/FSSpec regressions also pass, with 21 file-spec checks, 10 FSp checks and 17 catalogue checks including the new cases. Wrong module names previously caused the 68K CODE-reuse halt ([#4486](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4486)); the merged name fixes resolve its bounded startup reproduction. [TempTopMem support #4487](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4487) is also merged. No jump-offset bypass, opcode patch, or archive rename was used.

With both merged path fixes, untouched 68K startup reaches the original English registration notice at 600 frontend / 1200 guest ticks, zero exhaustion. Its offered Not Yet option opens the welcome/setup flow. [VisibleLength PR #4495](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4495), head `8385a1f95ec193308ece6e2c6fa0ef7ec9932e91`, additionally resolves the unhandled `$84080028` text call: its integrated ABI regression and all 19 focused ScriptUtil tests pass, and the fresh native build succeeds. Original-archive replay reaches welcome/setup at 916 frontend / 1500 guest ticks with no remaining selector warning; all exact-head CI checks passed, and the PR is merged as `72a41d73706ad2fac832f754bcbbef90298fbcdc`.

The initial 68K welcome/setup attempts showed credits/help over Game Setup, and Begin Game clicks did not advance. [Investigation #4496](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4496) records the traces, but later normal interaction changes that diagnosis: clicking the credits and closing the repeated welcome exposes a usable Game Setup. Begin Game opens the original New Galaxy save dialog, and Save reaches Game Login at 2164 frontend / 2732 guest ticks, zero exhaustion and zero assertions. The inspected login capture is partly blank/overdrawn; no gameplay qualification follows. Original click-handler disassembly also shows it rejects clicks into another window when the front application window has a modal flag. Do not treat the earlier overlay alone as proof of a visibility/clipping defect, or suppress legitimate modal drawing. [ShowHide Boolean ABI fix #4499](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4499) is merged with all 215 window regressions passing. The unchanged original replay still reaches partly blank Game Login and returns to a save panel, at 2412 frontend / 2980 guest ticks, zero exhaustion and zero assertions. Further login/gameplay and browser evidence remain pending.

PowerPC resolves all five enabled original language modules through FSMakeFSSpec and FSpOpenResFile, then stops on unsupported `InterfaceLib:IUStringOrder` at PC `$01F002EC`, SP `$07F6F8C0`, LR `$0102A35C` ([#4493](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4493)). Both architectures still need bounded gameplay, browser captures, launch approval and public verification; unhosted.

## Yahtzee! 3.2.0 — target 100, original package intake

Inspected 11 October 2026. The [archive index](https://www.vintageapplemac.com/software/games/y/) links [Yahtzee! v3.2.0.sit](https://www.vintageapplemac.com/files/games/Yahtzee%21%20v3.2.0.sit): 25,001 bytes, SHA-256 `a6918a4776377cfca37c9be377ccc0c79591fb8927b3a70098a7e400a8147c28`. Independent unar and production extraction byte-match both files / all four forks, with original filenames retained. The original resource-only application has CODE 0 and 1, no cfrg, and identifies version 3.2.0 (October 1995), Mikael Sheikh / Necromancer Software. This package provides a 68K slice; no PPC slice was located.

The original accompanying readme explicitly permits redistribution by any means if it accompanies the unmodified application, neither file is edited, and charges stay within reasonable reproduction costs. It separately reserves sale, incorporation into other products, and commercial exploitation for prior written consent. Preserve both original files, optional compensation/source-request notices, and these conditions. Any proposed delivery must remain free, noncommercial distribution of the original complete package, separate from the emulator binary; no incorporation permission or modified-software grant is inferred.

Native with merged date/TempTopMem/archive-component support, selector `Yahtzee! v3.2.0/Yahtzee!`, explicit 68K, depth 8: Cmd-N and paced original player setup open the scorepad/dice windows at 1215 frontend / 1815 guest ticks. Selecting dice enables Roll, and a Roll advances the reroll prompt. Clicking the player score column under Chance commits 22, with Lower Total and Grand Total 22 and the matching status message, at 2535 / 3119 ticks. Actual captures inspected, zero exhaustion, zero assertions. All five visible dice still show sixes, which would total 30 ([rendering issue #4492](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4492)); gameplay qualification remains held.

Original ICON 2001–2006 and 2011–2016 contain distinct correct normal/selected face images. CODE 1's roll/draw routine stores its returned die value and directly calls PlotIcon at `$2BB4`, without an intervening erase; its redraw loop calls PlotIcon at `$2FF0`. A bounded CopyBits trace reports no CopyBits calls; an EraseRect trace logs status/title/scorepad rectangles without proving every drawing operation. [Original drawing evidence](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4492#issuecomment-6104695068) narrows diagnosis without establishing its cause. Apple Technical Note 055 describes srcOr, while Inside Macintosh I I-473 and the current source comment describe srcCopy; resolve this discrepancy before changing transfer semantics. The stopped entropy trace never reached Roll and supplies no random-call evidence. Distribution review, corrected visible gameplay, bounded assertions and browser validation remain pending; unhosted.


12 October 2026 icon-redraw follow-up: a fresh original-archive baseline from
native source `392e612c55387756ec217577f085b387948da491` reproduces the same
22-point mismatch at 2535 frontend / 3119 guest ticks. The original
[Inside Macintosh I scan](https://www.vintageapple.org/inside_o/pdf/Inside_Macintosh_Volume_I_1985.pdf),
printed I-473 / PDF page 485, was downloaded and visually inspected: it explicitly
specifies srcCopy. The conflicting older Technical Note 055 remains documented;
no independent Macintosh ROM oracle is claimed. The existing trap's comment
already promised srcCopy, but its body skipped blank source pixels and ORed
packed destination bits, accumulating earlier die markings.

Trial native source `400a685b6365aa226cb220bf7b8da61d4600bbbb` replaces blank
pixels in the existing 1-bit and 8-bit icon paths. All four focused icon tests
pass, including repeated filled-to-blank redraws and unchanged adjacent pixels
in both formats; the optimized executable build passes. The unchanged original
scenario now displays the correct disabled-face ICONs 2025/2026/2024/2025/2022
before scoring: faces 5+6+4+5+2 total 22. Every pixel of those five 32×32 icons
matches the corresponding original resource bitmap. Chance, Lower Total and
Grand Total then show 22. The next turn displays normal ICONs
2006/2005/2001/2004/2006, also independently bitmap-matched; these are new-turn
faces and must not be confused with the scored roll. Fresh bounded replay
passes 152 checks of original icon pixels and measured score changes at
2535 frontend / 3119 guest ticks, zero exhausted frames; captures inspected.
This qualifies one 68K scoring turn, not a full match, saves, audio or independent
ROM fidelity. [Compatibility PR #4662](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4662)
passed all nine exact-head CI checks at `3802c17b90b77b0ed05313bbb3f80bfde7e100e4`
and merged as `186841d43e139d94a775986325f039973d20cb1b` on 11 October 2026
20:33:19 UTC. Both 4×/1× icon reference corrections were independently derived
from the fixture bitmap, with no differences outside the blank icon pixels.
Distribution delivery review, hosted browser qualification, catalogue approval,
human release and public replay remain pending. No release was triggered. No hosted archive, catalogue entry or live-qualified increment.

## MacSolitaire 1.6 — target 614, original installer intake

Inspected 11 October 2026. [Original archive](https://www.vintageapplemac.com/files/games/MacSolitaire%201.6%20Freeware.sit), discovered in the [M index](https://www.vintageapplemac.com/software/games/m/): 684,704 bytes, SHA-256 `80bdb1c81e0375bba53eb2586fa2686a1be353ef4e58ec04dccdf259c5da49df`. The outer application is StuffIt InstallerMaker 7.0.1, not the game itself. Independent unar expansion and Systemless production loading identify the installed `MacSolitaire 1.6 Freeware/MacSolitaire` application with 929,597 data-fork / 606,632 resource-fork bytes. A bytewise comparison now confirms that the production pack preserves both installed game forks exactly against independent unar expansion. Single- and multi-source production packing are byte-identical at 1,536,295 bytes. This validates extraction, not rights or browser execution.

The installed original vers resource identifies MacSolitaire 1.6 by PCV enr. Its data fork begins `Joy!peffpwpc`, with a `cfrg` fragment and no CODE resources: this edition is PowerPC-only. Do not infer 68K game support from the installer's CODE resources. Preserve the complete original installer package. Its freeware filename is not a redistribution grant; embedded installer TEXT resources are ADCR-compressed, and applicable distribution terms remain uninspected. Rights are held, and the archive is unhosted.

Selector `MacSolitaire`, explicit PowerPC, depth 8. The [grow-zone #4502](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4502), [Internet Config #4505](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4505), [CarbonLib object support #4509](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4509), [OSL descriptor #4513](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4513) and [menu-query #4519](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4519) fixes are merged after their tests, native builds and full CI passed. The OSL family has two integrated tests across eight constructor/disposal families and sixteen exports plus 62 Mixed Mode regressions; the menu query has two integrated tests plus all 98 menu regressions. Original-archive replay from exact head `7ed1493acba793d2b616868e0b21cb578755b8e6` advances beyond IsMenuItemEnabled, then halts on weak CarbonLib `GetGlobalMouse`, symbol 79, TOC slot `013C`, reached through original stub `010B90D4` (PC zero, LR `01012D18`, SP `07F6FDB0`, 63,456 cycles). The bounded 600-tick request fails at frontend 1 / guest 0, zero exhaustion/assertions and no capture. Applicable library source obligations and soundtrack/component terms remain to be satisfied before complete-package hosting; the program grant alone is not a completed clearance. The original main GLTron application has 106,446 data bytes beginning `Joy!peffpwpc` and 166,362 resource bytes, with cfrg and no CODE: this edition is PPC-only. The ReadMe requires classic Mac OS 8.1+, DrawSprocket and OpenGL. Original selector GLTron, PowerPC preference, depth 8, bounded 600-tick attempt on exact head `39bd994b91a4fb2ba38886c0da73f246a9fa8464` fails at classification with No executable found, before guest startup. The cfrg resource is 84 bytes: header 32 and final record 52, while memberSize declares 56. Extension count is zero and the name is GL Tron. The official release has the identical resource fork. Apple CodeFragments.h documents memberSize including extensions; determine original CFM handling before changing the parser. Do not patch the archive or broadly accept truncated fragment records. [Intake and loader investigation #4517](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4517) remains open. Native gameplay, browser capture, promotion, launch approval and public replay remain pending.

Applicable Mac port/component permission and complete-package delivery compliance remain unresolved, so hosting approval stays pending. The different registered-game licence does not replace these original shareware terms.


Heretic bounded gameplay now [repeats with twelve measured assertions](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4563#issuecomment-6107484161), using unchanged 0.9.2, original Heretic PPC selector, explicit PPC/depth 8, native executable built from `2a4c8e13267819e66405b140bde8cd00461db16f`. Ordinary original menus select City of the Damned/Bringest Them Oneth, then Escape closes the remaining menu. Held Up-arrow moves forward and collects a wand crystal (ammo 50→60); held Control fires (60→58). Ready/move/fire captures inspected. Replay passes frontend 1,150/guest 1,150, zero exhaustion. Scene/ammo-digit pixels are measured from observed original captures, not an independent emulator oracle. The later captures have plausible colours, so the initial palette finding does not prove a persistent all-game failure; original 0.9.3 notes describe a colour workaround. Full-level outcomes, audio, saving/loading, browser behaviour, complete Mac port/component permission and production parity remain pending. No hosting or public-live approval.

## NetHack 3.3.1 — reserve R62

[Intake #4568](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4568). This well-known classic game replaces the non-game WadTyper reserve lead, not a primary slot. [Official publisher fat-binary page](https://www.nethack.org/v331/ports/download-mac.html) explicitly permits distribution and identifies 68K/PPC. Unchanged [binary archive](https://www.nethack.org/download/3.3.1/nethack-331-fat.sit), 1,980,242 bytes, SHA-256 `45aac1a040ec7d915cff92f4f3142bbc3a2741a3b377535cab25906f9dc57e27`; MD5 `926b060770b82368fb4f968142365945` matches the publisher receipt. Unchanged [corresponding source](https://www.nethack.org/download/3.3.1/nethack-331.tgz), 3,061,094 bytes, SHA-256 `09632223a395a4b792f00c9d209045254e3f6a7dec45bf2ce17454a2d12db606`; MD5 `d0600ce4698c80e5ad1f917ded1c36d2` matches the [official source receipt](https://www.nethack.org/v331/download-src.html).

Independent source extraction has 575 original files, including sys/mac code/resources/project data. Bundled binary License and source dat/license are both 4,875 bytes and match after original newline normalization. NetHack General Public License permits executable redistribution with complete source or its permitted source-information alternative. Retain the complete package/notices and deliver corresponding source alongside the binary. This affirmative original-package evidence is distinct from inferring permissions for an unrelated port.

Actual game: 1,765,580-byte PPC Joy!peff data fork; independently decoded AppleDouble yields a 2,284,081-byte resource fork, two CODE and one cfrg. Both are game slices. Keep resource-only Recover companion, documentation/defaults/licence and Finder metadata intact. Production all-file fork/Finder comparison remains pending.

Native exact executable built from `79da62287c8a1608e02da3003c2b4d2b77123e1f`, original sit/selector NetHack/depth 8: explicit 68K startup opens the original record file then fails with illegal opcode `0x1DC0` at PC `0x0076752C`. Explicit PPC startup completes 600 frontend/600 guest ticks, zero exhaustion/assertions, reaching the original name/role/race character prompt with layout defects. Ordinary Play click at h520/v158 opens an original error alert reporting -50 rather than a playable dungeon. Its 1,202-tick bounded script completes but has zero assertions; capture visually inspected, so this is a qualification failure despite the runner's passed status. Diagnose both architecture paths; do not patch the archive or suppress the alert. Gameplay, browser input/captures, asset promotion/hosting, launch approval, human release and public replay remain pending. No catalogue entry or new live game is counted.

### Original 3.3.1 resource probe and 3.4.3 Carbon alternative

Further unmodified 3.3.1 PPC diagnostic: original `GetNamedResource` asks for `SAVE`, returns a null handle, and `ResError` returns zero. Original `OpenHandleFile` checks the error rather than the handle and continues. [Apple's Resource Manager reference](https://leopard-adc.pepas.com/documentation/Carbon/Reference/Resource_Manager/Reference/reference.html) explicitly documents null/noErr for a type absent from all open maps; this observation does not justify changing Systemless's result to force this game through startup. Ordinary alert OK leaves blank windows even after another 600 frontend ticks (755 frontend/guest total, zero assertions/exhaustion; captures inspected). [Trace evidence](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4568#issuecomment-6107728043).

The [publisher's 3.4.3 Macintosh page](https://www.nethack.org/v343/ports/download-mac.html) also supplies an original classic-Mac PPC Carbon edition, covering Mac OS 8.5/9.x. It is an alternative edition of the same NetHack reserve candidate, not a second distinct game or a replacement claim for the still-failing 68K slice. Unchanged [binary](https://www.nethack.org/download/3.4.3/nethack-343-mac-carbon.sit): 1,083,366 bytes, SHA-256 `37b103f7468539c92bcf86521df91a656618a3d9c3359a0e91c979323d08efd1`, publisher-matching MD5 `7125425d3644ad962a330db45fa88473`. Unchanged [upstream source](https://www.nethack.org/download/3.4.3/nethack-343-src.tgz): 3,497,458 bytes, SHA-256 `bb39c3d2a9ee2df4a0c8fdde708fbc63740853a7608d2f4c560b488124866fe4`, MD5 `21479c95990eefe7650df582426457f9` matches the [source receipt](https://www.nethack.org/v343/download-src.html). Independent extraction yields 658 source files. Bundled License matches upstream dat/license after newline normalization.

The complete binary package includes original `src-diff.txt`, 33,631 bytes, SHA-256 `2e1540a7e6c8222a7f904cb0181b834b4894e1c21969be735eb03100ce5b7c9b`, identifying Kevin Hugo's port changes from official 3.4.3. Retain those original differences alongside upstream source and licence notices. Eight original data files include game, Guidebook, License, MacHelp, NetHack Defaults, data, quest.dat and src-diff.txt. Game data is 2,061,195 bytes with Joy!/peff/pwpc header; actual resource fork is 549,374 bytes, one cfrg and no CODE. Other nonempty forks are Guidebook 410, License 332, Defaults 410 and src-diff.txt 410 bytes. Production packing independently matches all eight original files/sixteen forks and each file’s Finder type/creator/flags. Complete pack is 3,106,761 bytes, SHA-256 `881d37c63709e1bee4be85c278d8aba22a0f455bbb7cf333702fa724377d6c6b`. Retain the unchanged upstream source, original port differences and notices with any distribution.

The SetMenuID, port bitmap/visible-region, window constraint and user-item/modal-filter callback fixes are merged through [PR #4596](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4596). Fresh native `fce52f2b823aee9e202dbb0bb8b752d02376b13e`, unchanged 3.4.3 archive, selector NetHack, PPC/depth 8 completes 600 frontend/guest ticks and reaches the original character-creation dialog, with zero exhaustion and zero gameplay assertions. Ordinary Play first exposes unsupported GetCaretTime; [fix #4600](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4600) is merged after all nine checks passed. Fresh native `bb44b06df45abb257f6709775c610af5e010be6c` passes that import and completes the Play interaction at 1,202 frontend/guest ticks, zero exhaustion/assertions, but the inspected original output reports initialization failure and “Another game in progress?”. Typing Player normally reproduces the same result at 1,226 ticks.

Independent original PEF decoding identifies HCreate import 111 and its original CFM stub at `0x011AA59C`. Bounded guest-stub/caller traces show the LEVL creation passing working-directory reference 32 and directory 19, then returning `nsvErr` -35 at `0x01191664`. HCreate formerly rejected registered working-directory references before creation; HOpen already resolved them. [Fix #4603](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4603) is merged after all nine checks passed, preserving exclusive-create and error semantics; its regression passes in ordinary and debugger library suites. Fresh integrated native source `c1a98415f47e04887b7afb0c4d8b302cfd5bfea4`, unchanged publisher archive, selector NetHack/PPC/depth 8, ordinary typing Player and clicking Play, then Space and Return, reaches the original dungeon. Keypad 4 moves the player and produces “You displaced your little dog.” Eight measured before/after pixel assertions pass at 1,532 frontend/guest ticks with zero exhaustion; captures inspected. [Original-game evidence](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4568#issuecomment-6109264960). This proves bounded first-dungeon movement for 3.4.3 PPC only. Broader gameplay, saves, audio and the 3.3.1 architecture failures remain unqualified. Browser qualification, catalogue approval, promotion/hosting, launch, human release and public replay remain pending. No new entry or qualified-live increment.


## Tetris Max 2.8 — target 275, primary slot 87

[Original intake #4456](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4456#issuecomment-6108056579). This resolves the earlier archive-download blocker for version 2.8. The author's modern page links to Macintosh Garden; an author-hosted original download has not been identified.

Independent extraction identifies sixteen original files, including resource-only registration/high-score tools, documents, backgrounds, pieces and both music files. Actual game data fork is 95,263 bytes, SHA-256 `320487ae033ae405793eccf79929cbd0ba08fc13a6f92f5ecabf97cd7dbb423c`, with `Joy!/peff/pwpc`. Actual game resource fork is 376,830 bytes, SHA-256 `f04a2636e7c8084c18c7c0815c9d087bee0e2107839c2c88975c1b8024337e79`; it includes cfrg 0 (96 bytes), substantial CODE 1 (33,372 bytes) and CODE 3 (21,120 bytes), plus CODE 0 (24 bytes). Both 68K and PPC are genuine game slices. Keep the complete original archive, forks and metadata; production packing parity remains pending.

Bundled About and game TEXT 6001 identify $10 shareware with registration requirements; no explicit redistribution grant has been established from the inspected documents. Game TEXT 6000 credits Steve Chamberlin / Opinicus Microtech, copyright 1996. Music STR# 129 identifies Peter Wagner as an original composer; Animal Instinct credits Jonathan Slatter, copyright 1995, and states it is used with permission. That documents licensed inclusion in the game, without independently establishing hosting permission. Full distribution scope remains pending. Preserve original registration and assets; do not strip music or unlock features to qualify the package.

Native baseline `2a4c8e13267819e66405b140bde8cd00461db16f`, unchanged archive, selector Tetris Max 2.8, explicit 68K/depth 8: original registration prompt appears. Ordinary Not Yet reaches the title/score screen at frontend 842 / guest 1,442 ticks, zero exhausted frames, zero assertions, terminal success. Inspected File menu offers Begin New Game. Ordinary File → Begin New Game additionally reaches a falling-piece board, next-piece preview and level/score counters at frontend 1,084 / guest 1,664 ticks, zero exhaustion and zero assertions, terminal success; capture inspected. Movement/rotation/drop and measured gameplay assertions remain unverified. PPC execution, browser validation, screenshot qualification, asset promotion, launch approval, human release and public replay remain pending. No catalogue entry or qualified-live increment.


## Gryversi 1.0.1c — additional discovery

[Intake #4584](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4584). The [author](https://www.gryphel.com/c/sw/games/gryversi/index.html) explicitly permits GPL version 2 redistribution. The unchanged [publisher ZIP](https://www.gryphel.com/d/sw/games/gryversi/c/gryversi-1.0.1c.zip) is 45,947 bytes, SHA-256 `54e31dd314cce3b669247af6533e2972994f29cb49b78c7f54572c950eb1f44e`; its MD5 `e9baccabcb96a8f8a7f8cbd43f7b2e1d` matches the author receipt, without independent signature verification. The original 819,200-byte HFS image MD5 `c7b9e146a749b91198afafab3a46adac` matches its bundled receipt.

Retain all six original HFS files: app.c, COPYING, Desktop, Documentation, Gryversi and README, plus the ZIP checksum receipt. The author supplies C source and GPLv2 licence in the same package as the game. Independent machfs extraction (with normally hidden Desktop retained) and production packing agree on all twelve forks, Finder type/creator/flags and every supported original volume metadata field. The loader names the guest volume Gryversi rather than original Untitled. No guest bytes were changed. Original game resource fork is 19,890 bytes, SHA-256 `a529e01eeef8135713faaefa1d39c5f3858dd927216057642bfcde5f31420e56`, CODE 0 is 24 bytes and CODE 1 is 15,996 bytes; data fork is empty and there is no cfrg. This is a genuine 68K edition, not a native PPC game. HyperCard documentation is preserved; its player is not bundled.

Exact native source `25bd439d950f84431d2d984eca5242a22b31ad49`, unchanged ZIP, selector Gryversi, 68K/depth 8: ordinary Time → 1 Second, then a legal White move and computer Black reply. Eight measured before/after pixel assertions pass at 754 frontend / 1,334 guest ticks, zero exhausted frames; captures inspected. A content-only grid capture accompanies the proposed entry. Full matches, saving, audio and documentation rendering remain unverified. Normal CI promotion preserved the complete original package and gameplay screenshot. Optimized Chrome preview from promoted source `e30e20a2d7824c9318566beb6e317f31e4ecf512`, locally enabled, runs an isolated 800x600 68K worker/WebGL at 25 MHz and at most two ticks per paint. Local frontend responses are controlled under the production origin; the actual managed archive request is untouched and HTTP 200, with the original hash and 45,947-byte body verified. Ordinary Time → 1 Second and White placement produce the flip and computer reply; eight measured before/after pixel checks pass and captures are inspected. Browser launch approved. [Catalogue PR #4585](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4585) merged with launch enabled. Published [v0.97.0](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/releases/tag/v0.97.0) and its [complete pipeline](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/actions/runs/38246494927) succeed, including website deployment, six native platforms and twelve nonempty package/checksum assets. Direct public Worker/Canvas2D replay on [systemless.org/gryversi/](https://systemless.org/gryversi/), with no preview frontend or archive fixture, selects the observed **1 Second** menu row. White plays zero-based row 2, column 4, flips Black at row 3, column 4; computer Black replies at row 4, column 5 and flips row 4, column 4. All **eight fixed before/after checks** pass; captures inspected. Pacing is **58.438 guest ticks/sec**, above the unchanged minimum 50, with no console errors. Fresh public ZIP GET matches **45,947 bytes** and the original SHA-256. This is the **eighteenth independently qualified new live game**; baseline audit remains open. Full matches, saving, audio and documentation rendering remain unverified. No release was triggered by this task.

### Rogue browser pacing candidate — 13 October 2026

[Compatibility #4755](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4755),
tested head `de839eb9a90e4f1a3c8e7b8f4d7044fab3abdd07`, admits null,
non-sleeping WaitNextEvent calls to exact CPU/memory identity proofs and retains
bounded observations between the browser's existing 64-instruction slices.
Host input/task changes and frame composition revoke unfinished observations;
sleeping calls and delivered events reject the proof. CPU speed, slice length,
time budget, original archive and pacing gate remain unchanged. All nine checks
passed on that exact head; the ordinary squash merge is
`1440d1368794ee60ffcd82043f072438416bbc80`.

All 80 idle tests and 17 event tests pass. Final native replay passes eight
movement pixels plus two full-image regression comparisons: 151 frontend /
751 guest ticks, zero exhausted frames, both captures pixel-identical to the
preserved Systemless baseline. The immutable native binary SHA-256 is
`b2254088fc7aed31eb9e32623a46baa53804ca3a5afd854213c9e79b60dc1a1a`.
This is a regression comparison, not an independent emulator oracle.

Two fresh private Chrome 151 / Worker / Canvas2D runs fetch the unchanged hosted
original HQX at default 25 MHz and pass at 60.00356 / 60.04722 guest ticks/sec,
with no console errors. Both pass eight measured player-pixel checks and exact
one-cell-left glyph matching on different dungeon layouts; captures inspected.
The fixed browser glyph reference comes from the preserved human v0.97.0
public replay. These private frontend bridges do not qualify the public site.

The earlier native-only candidate still failed browser pacing at 26.441; its
failure is retained. Galactic Frontiers still reaches Turn 2 but fails at
30.51525 on the revised candidate, so [#4747](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4747)
stays open. The fix is merged; Rogue requires a human-triggered release and
direct public pacing/gameplay verification. Broader dungeon progression,
combat, saving and audio remain unverified. No qualified-live increment or
agent release action.


## MacCheckers 1.0 — primary slot 779

[Intake #4618](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4618).
The [publisher’s classic software page](https://www.dejal.com/classic/ancient/)
links the original B&W edition and a separate colour edition. The inspected B&W
[publisher HQX](https://www.dejal.com/download/classic/mac-checkers.hqx) is
26,454 bytes, SHA-256
`0486d463119b9fc1eaf1f9001ccccde6a4f18af99aa686725d30a8e2b9d3af9a`.
It is byte-identical to the
[original-format preservation copy](https://www.gryphel.com/d/sw/games/checkers/c/mac-checkers.hqx).
MD5 `c8d377e9cdda46bd4ab0f7e6a865394e` matches the
[published preservation receipt](https://www.gryphel.com/c/sw/games/checkers/index.html);
its signature was not independently verified. The archive is Compact Pro inside
BinHex; the repackaged HFS ZIP is not used.

Independent unar yields MacCheckers 1.0 and six Sample Games. All seven files,
fourteen forks and supported Finder type/creator/flags exactly match production
packing (35,917 bytes). The application is resource-only, with three CODE
resources and no cfrg or PPC slice. The colour edition has not been inspected and
would not be a second distinct game.

Rights remain held. The publisher labels the download freeware, but its
[legal page](https://www.dejal.com/legal/) reserves rights. Inspected original
notices contain author credits and no affirmative redistribution grant. Freeware
availability does not establish permission for Systemless to mirror this archive.
No catalogue entry, hosted assets, launch approval or author contact follows from
this intake.

On native source `fbd12e1e33941e1da82b9ceb61ed28966571ed81`, selector MacCheckers
1.0, explicit 68K/depth 8, the unchanged original reaches Black/White selection.
Ordinary Black selection reaches Black to move status and the checkerboard shell,
but no pieces appear in the inspected capture. No legal move or computer reply is
qualified. Saves, browser, promotion and public replay remain unverified.
[Original comparison and startup evidence](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4618#issuecomment-6109947535).

`727898fb535d5fed055d3f58225ff8187c5462adcfe48b454746a63dce2296fe`.

Possajennikov, states that the program is free, and permits source use in any
program without limitations. Preserve the custom permission text and all sources.
Applicability to public redistribution of the bundled executable/resource artwork
remains under assessment; no hosting approval is inferred solely from freeware.

matches all fourteen forks and Finder type/creator/flags against production

normalize the forks. The source-package fork remains SHA-256
`381a1ebff704cd5ca6a5ed09e4128aa4ea69369f8537520b9f351f8f5b371bbb`.

On native `fbd12e1e33941e1da82b9ceb61ed28966571ed81`, selector Kalah, explicit
68K/depth 8, ordinary Game → Start and a lower-row pit click produce a human move,
computer response and return to Your move. Both stores change 0→1; eight measured
checks pass at 786 frontend / 1,364 guest ticks with zero exhaustion. Original
source confirms the lower-row hit area despite the About diagram’s upper-row user
label. Captures inspected.
[Bounded gameplay](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4621#issuecomment-6110050761),
[complete preservation comparison](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4621#issuecomment-6110067754).
Full match, levels, saves, audio and browser remain unverified; unhosted, no live

includes both merged fixes and used a local launch flag for testing before
approval. Actual managed browser requests were untouched; cross-origin isolation,

walking and A ladder climbing pass sixteen measured before/after pixel checks;

PR, merged at `1463e21ee247d07ac223ba205df3fb50b5a25585` after all final checks
passed. Human v0.97.0 release and [direct public walking/climbing replay](#spacestation-pheta-direct-public-replay--qualified-bounded-gameplay)

Primary target 83; [intake #4470](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4470).
Fresh inspection on 12 October 2026 identifies the unchanged
[4.1 archive](https://www.vintageapplemac.com/files/games/MacBrickout%204.1.sit):
2,236,189 bytes, SHA-256
`4eb9de6206f205da11a8852e5a8a38b4433d635a5835dd769ad8021067ad3508`.
Independent `unar -forks fork` extraction and production loading agree on all
109 files, all 218 data/resource forks and each file's Finder type, creator and
flags, with no missing paths or mismatches. Packed size is 5,830,997 bytes.

The original main data fork is `Joy!peffpwpc`; its resource fork is 1,402,458
bytes, contains one `pwpc` cfrg member and no CODE resources. Selecting 68K still
loads PPC and is not a second architecture success. The separate 3.1 package
has CODE resources and a recorded illegal-opcode failure in the intake; that
older failure has not been replayed against this new runtime. Both versions
represent one distinct game. Original TEXT 128 retains the demo/shareware
registration terms. No affirmative public-hosting grant has been established;
all package content remains unhosted.

Exact selector `MacBrickout 4.1/MacBrickout 4.1`, explicit PPC/depth 8, native
`0f24e29c473c5ed912fa7cca50be45e38698ae11` halts at frontend 35 / guest 34,
PC zero, LR `010112E4`, before capture. Original call `010112E0` enters stub
`01046BBC`, TOC slot `0030`, optional import 12,
`DSpContext_GetDirtyRectGridUnits`. The original also imports the grid setter.
[Dirty-grid fix #4634](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4634)
merged as `816f96046924b6bd3709d1231351f1987f55a68f`, after 69 focused tests
and all nine final ordinary CI checks passed. Fresh immutable native
`a611f6959ff693fa6cc79e3070f033fc5c7f676c` advances through the old halt,
then fails at frontend 45 / guest 44, PC zero, LR `01011330`, SP `07F6FE70`.
Original call `0101132C` enters `01046BEC`, TOC slot `0048`, optional import 18,
`DSpContext_GetMonitorFrequency`. Zero exhausted frames and zero assertions
are not startup/gameplay qualification.
[Monitor fix #4637](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4637) and
[handle-growth fix #4642](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4642)
are merged, each with all nine CI/CLA checks passing. The monitor regression
suite passed 71 tests. Fresh clean combined native `2ad262dc` contains the
reviewed monitor query and in-place handle-growth fix; the original PPC 4.1
replay reaches frontend 348 / guest 347 with zero exhausted frames. Its inspected
failure capture displays the original title screen, then the guest halts at PC
zero / LR `010119DC`, SP `07F6FB50`. This is progress beyond the earlier 45-tick
boundary, not gameplay qualification or a numerical speedup measurement. A broad instruction
trace changed the execution path and was stopped; it is excluded from gameplay
evidence. Rights, native gameplay, browser approval and public delivery remain
held. Only a human may trigger a release.


[Alternate-buffer invalidation fix #4655](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4655)
merged as `cdc7ab13318688816770977c1041445c2e683d5b` after all nine CI/CLA
checks passed. All 70 focused DrawSprocket tests pass, including underlay pixels,
disjoint-region gap preservation, presentation before sprite restoration and

`392e612c55387756ec217577f085b387948da491`, same unchanged 4.1 original and
explicit PPC/depth 8 selector, advances beyond the prior tick-348 optional-import
halt to the original registration dialog. The diagnostic completes 1,080
frontend/guest ticks with no exhaustion; zero assertions prove only completion
of its requested actions. Inspected captures show a blank central registration
text area with controls and scrollbar rendered. The trace still reports PC-zero /
LR-zero callback halts with r12 `01E003D8` / `01E003F0`; these are not fixed by
this scoped invalidation repair. A separate fresh ordinary OK dismissal (v423,

`d0ec69adf03ded191801f45992757398a742a208cf6beb49cc0824166bcbaca0`.

`bac67e164d7d3955ae52064d2cb434298a3aaa62c8247c7d9898802efdda79ab`.

Original TEXT 5011 identifies Ken Winograd and suggests a ten-dollar donation;

[author's site](https://www.winograd.com/aboutkenwinograd.shtml) corroborates

before clicking. A follow-up using the inspected File menu's New Game - 1 Paddle

with zero exhaustion/assertions; its inspected captures likewise show no visible

All archives, extraction receipts, scripts and captures remain outside Git.
Distribution permission, bounded visible paddle/ball control and brick
collisions, content-only screenshot, managed-archive browser qualification,

pending. These inspections change neither catalogue entry count nor qualified
live count.

## Re-Pete 2.2 original package and sequence playback

Primary target 613; [intake #4647](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4647).
The [publisher](https://www.tex-edit.com/index.html#Re-Pete) links Classic 2.2,
separately from its OS X edition. Its About Our Freeware section explicitly
permits distribution with documentation intact and programs unchanged, while
excluding beta reposts. This release archive qualifies under that conditional
grant; a freeware label alone would not suffice. The complete resource fork,
including embedded instructions, remains intact.

The [publisher archive](https://www.tex-edit.com/software/re-pete2.2.sit.hqx) and
[Gryphel preservation copy](https://www.gryphel.com/d/sw/games/repete/c/re-pete2.2.sit.hqx)
are byte-identical: 73,613 bytes, SHA-256
`8de6839796c93ba71a2a9f800a3e2d002f780f8e84356b717ef8c6e4e415add9`.
MD5 `0868ad780f4c7b40c7b32b8f4711f7d8` matches the preservation receipt; its
signature has not been independently verified. Independent `unar` extraction
and production packing match the single application's two forks and Finder
type, creator and flags. The data fork is empty; the resource fork is 71,628
bytes. CODE 0/1/3 are present; there is no cfrg or native PPC executable. The
intact production pack is 71,675 bytes. This edition is **68K only**; the OS X
edition is not another classic game or architecture qualification.

Selector `Re-Pete 2.2/Re-Pete`, explicit 68K/depth 8, immutable native
`81788cd9974cb1de44b8d6c9c24bf2e36b7d4fd1`: ordinary Start Game and button 5
input produce Score 0→1 in a repeated run with eight measured pixel assertions,
336 frontend / 912 guest ticks and zero exhausted frames. A matching button 8
probe remains at Score 0. These checks do not establish complete memory-game
playability.

A longer no-input diagnostic captures every frame for 300 ticks after menu
selection: 424 total frontend / 1,002 guest ticks, zero exhausted frames. Control
and sound traces show the first button-5 sequence highlight and clear both occur
inside frontend tick 249. The original calls square-wave `freqDurationCmd` 40,
duration 250 half-milliseconds, MIDI note 68; the adapter logs it as unhandled.
The original then passes a false Boolean using `CLR.B -(SP)` to
SndDisposeChannel. Its unused padding byte is 106, which the current word read
mistakes for true; disposal also unconditionally releases the channel instead
of draining it. The first 100-frame diagnostic ended before the highlight and
must not be interpreted as proof that the original never called HiliteControl.
[Compatibility defect #4649](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4649)
tracks note playback, Boolean decoding and scheduler-based draining disposal.
Repaired native build `1df6ea6dc2b5deab2f3466134b123ac308f13a70` now holds the
first flash across eight frontend captures. A diagnostic measured 2,761 non-silent
PCM samples during that note interval; its deliberately unreachable assertion was
measurement only. A fresh bounded two-round scenario passes 16 measured pixel/audio
checks across 530 frontend / 1,102 guest ticks with no exhaustion. Ordinary matching
input reproduces the first 5, then the next sequence 5 and 7, advancing score 0→1→3.
Captures were inspected before assertions were added. This is bounded native gameplay,
not independent audio fidelity or exhaustive difficulty qualification. [Repair #4657](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4657)
merged as `d5b3864bc4f1f9d59e2493fd41f8489041a004ec` after all nine ordinary CI/CLA
checks passed, including normal/debug library and desktop tests, structured headless
play, 68K/PPC showcase assertions and packaging. The sound/runner implementation
matches the replayed binary; later default-branch utilities are being replayed in
the integrated browser preview.

A disabled catalogue intake now records the exact publisher archive and a content-only
score-3 gameplay screenshot. [Intake #4656](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4656) promoted the
unchanged archive and content-only screenshot to immutable Systemless assets.
Production validation passes all 213 proposed entries. A fresh hosted archive
fetch is 73,613 bytes with the original SHA-256 and byte-identical to the publisher
file. Screenshot SHA-256 is `9f126808b4d5b80c37741d72ee20bfd06d335491b5d8d0f3c68174062c6561ac`
(6,040 bytes). Bounded browser preview is verified below. Integrated-runtime replay, launch approval, human release
and independent public replay remain pending; there is no qualified live-count
increment.


Browser preview `052693c3` combines the promoted entry with the rebased sound
repair and uses a local-only launch override. Actual Chrome 151.0.7922.174 loads
an untouched hosted archive request: HTTP 200, 73,613 bytes, SHA-256 matching the
publisher original; browser Network.getResponseBody verifies these actual bytes.
Only frontend files are substituted at the production origin. WebGL, runtime
worker, cross-origin isolation, 800×600 canvas and default 25 MHz are verified.
A separate diagnostic captures the first button-5 highlight golden, then cleared.
Ordinary retained-menu drag and held matching clicks complete two rounds and
advance score 0→1→3. All 12 measured score-pixel checks pass in a fresh replay;
captures inspected, pacing probe passes and there are no console errors. The
initial localhost fetch does not qualify (production-origin CORS is required),
quick-click input never started the game, and a server-reset attempt ended before
assertions; those attempts are excluded. Difficulty coverage and browser audio

and independent public replay remain pending. No qualified-live increment yet.


Integrated browser source `4a861808896b4a6af6706ac1b64a652d6520afea` has an
identical runtime src tree to merged sound/text-utility head `d5b3864b`.
Fresh optimized production preview replays the unchanged 12 expected score-pixel
checks successfully through two rounds, score 0→1→3; actual browser archive GET

The score-3 capture is inspected and the pacing probe passes with no console

## On the Edge 3.1 — primary 508, complete original package

Inspected 12 October 2026. The [preservation index](https://www.gryphel.com/c/sw/games/edge/)
links the [original BinHex/StuffIt archive](https://www.gryphel.com/d/sw/games/edge/c/ontheedge.sit.hqx),
not its repackaged HFS disk. Original download: 22,676 bytes, SHA-256
`666d412e535e9be3d4b994be9ef1e04ec43ed77fa8a0820d7a877a9a4e80bde3`.
Production packing preserves both original files / all four forks exactly
against independent unar extraction, plus each file's Finder type, creator and
flags. Application: 15,058 data / 21,540 resource bytes. Accompanying PICT readme:
2,514 data / zero resource bytes. Complete production pack: 39,221 bytes.

The original PICT readme's text records were inspected, and its unmodified
file was independently rendered. It permits free copying and distribution
without charge, retains the five-dollar shareware request and copyright, and
reserves sold software libraries and commercial bundling for written consent.
This proposed free preservation delivery keeps the full original archive and
readme separate from the emulator binary. No sale, commercial-bundling or
modified-software grant is inferred. Actual executable has CODE 0/1/3, a
`Joy!peffpwpc` data fork and cfrg; vers identifies 3.1. Both game slices are
present. The preserved edition is dated 1995 by the archive index.

[Catalogue intake #4661](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4661):
immutable native `392e612c55387756ec217577f085b387948da491`, exact original archive,
selector `On the Edge 3.1/On the Edge`, explicit 68K/depth 8. Command-N starts
New game, then an ordinary inner-square click places the six of hearts, draws
an ace of clubs and changes Score 0 to 100. A fresh bounded replay passes twelve
measured before/after pixel checks at 786 frontend / 1,386 guest ticks, zero
exhaustion; captures inspected. This proves one placement, not completed games,
card removal, undo, saving or audio. A later Command-Z attempt made no visible
change and does not qualify undo. A content-only crop of the successful placement
is 3,228 bytes, SHA-256
`379b25620693cd71d32c8bd3289cf1f4fbb19b93973df969c30276c67d46d724`.

PPC baseline repeats on native `400a685b6365aa226cb220bf7b8da61d4600bbbb`: startup
halts at frontend 1 / guest 0, PC 0 / LR 0, SP `07F6FFC0`, 41,206 cycles, zero
assertions and no requested checkpoint; an empty framed window is inspected.
Original instruction/register traces show zero-extended negative Random values
produce negative shuffle indexes: result `0000EAC5` becomes `FFFF153B` after
negation, then remainder -41. A memory-write watch observes the guest's shuffle
store at `0100294C` repeatedly overwrite quit flag `0200047E`, ending with `1B`.

## MacTaipei 1.0 — primary 103 original package intake

[Intake #4670](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4670). Inspected 12 October 2026. The [requested archive index](https://www.vintageapplemac.com/software/games/m/) links the unchanged [MacTaipei 1.0.sit](https://www.vintageapplemac.com/files/games/MacTaipei%201.0.sit): **252,407 bytes**, SHA-256 `274ae5026a4b15bc3265c232ca160189268efc73fe6ba9402534c387b113cddc`. Independent unar expands the outer StuffIt 5 archive to a resource-bearing InstallerMaker wrapper with ST46 data, then independently expands that embedded archive to three original self-reading/game applications. Production packing emits 274,165 bytes and exactly matches all three installed files, six forks and supported Finder type/creator/flags. Preserve the original archive; no repacking or missing-documents substitution.

Actual installed `MacTaipei 1.0/MacTaipei` is resource-only, with CODE 0 (24 bytes), 1 (8,550) and 3 (196), no cfrg. This proves **68K only**, independently of the installer and document readers. The game version resource identifies 1.0 / F. Breukelman, 1997; its visible board copyright is 1998. Do not infer a native PPC game from the author's statement that it runs on some PowerPC Macs.

Both original self-reading documents were opened in Systemless and visually inspected, including the registration document's bottom. Readme describes a 68K solitaire Mahjong game, free-tile rules and matching pairs. Registration states a fully functional two-week shareware trial with $10 registration, reminders every ten minutes and after games; no registration bypass was used. The [original Info-Mac submission dated 28 August 1998](https://info-mac.org/viewtopic.php?f=213&sid=bf86a88dcea634fa1947df24c5c34623&t=10781) requests inclusion in Info-Mac internet archives and any CD-ROM. This is an affirmative, specifically scoped archival request; broader Systemless-hosting permission remains unresolved. The inspected document pages do not establish a broader distribution grant. No hosting or launch approval.

Native optimized revision `17dd70029960382d69cbb36ee9f4de277abbf028`, untouched archive, selector above, explicit 68K/depth 8: a 144-tile board opens. Ordinary clicks remove matching eight-character tiles at (331,68) and (200,198), revealing the underlying board and changing **144 to 142 tiles left**. Fresh replay passes ten measured before/after pixel assertions at **672 frontend / 1,272 guest ticks**, zero exhausted frames; captures inspected. The assertion set checks tile/board changes; the numeric count is established by capture inspection. Earlier nonmatching or blocked-tile probes did not qualify gameplay; Hint was inspected separately. Full board completion, undo, saves, audio, applicable complete-package hosting rights, browser qualification, catalogue CI/promotion, human release and public replay remain pending.

Separately, an exported diagnostic folder containing the original five files/forks/Finder metadata reaches the registration dialog. Ordinary DEMO, New Game and three computer-player selections deal a table. A player discard is rendered with computer responses and a visible claimed North-wind meld. Captures inspected; no gameplay assertions yet. This diagnostic input is explicitly not substituted for the untouched distribution. Both contain the same 93,121-byte Joy!/peff/pwpc game data and 207,142-byte resource fork with CODE 0–9 and cfrg: actual **68K and PPC**. The shareware edition retains original ReadMe, manual, data/sounds and both Icon files. Original ReadMe notes the author lacked a PPC and did not then support the native version.

Original self-reading manual's **Index → About Shareware → Software License** was visually inspected. Section 2 expressly permits non-profit distribution without prior/written notice when the software is unchanged and the complete package contents are included; for-profit distribution requires signed written permission. Retain copyright/proprietary notices. Section 3 requires registration after 30 days of evaluation. Intended scope is gratis non-profit preservation of the complete unchanged shareware distribution, separately from the emulator; no registration patch or key. Direct licence section capture is readable; scrolling the manual produces overlapping content in this runtime.

Production packing from native `f1110f94ca3f7492c360a0fa357659160b650504` emits **838,557 bytes** and independently matches **seven files/fourteen forks and supported Finder metadata** against unar. Exact selector `Powerball Shareware/Powerball.fat`; include the original manual, ReadMe, data/sound resource files and both Icon files.

Untouched shareware archive, native `e3366558e1a1094a36567c16df06ee0f214a030d`, explicit depth 8: PPC halts at unsupported `InterfaceLib:LMGetCurMap`, PC `$01F00188`, SP `$07F6FF00`, LR `$0100EE8C`. 68K completes the bounded 600-frontend checkpoint with zero exhaustion but a blank screen and zero assertions; gameplay is unqualified. These are baseline failures, not the current support state. Ordinary accessor PR [#4684](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4684) merged after all nine checks passed (merge `32861fbf9318930b68f55cb2045367483afe17df`); display Status/GetGamma PR #4685 also merged (`3405882741553d10ece4660ed904b90c47f3c06f`). Integrated native source `9a8e9a622e26dce1f6d91789adb49b4295dbfcce` reaches the original title on both slices. After a normal title-dismissal click and Cmd-N, 68K remains black and PPC halts at PC zero, SP `$07F6FC00`, LR `$01009F8C`, 19,904 cycles. The fresh 68K trace completes 972 frontend ticks / 1,572 guest ticks with zero exhaustion and zero assertions: it proves the observed failure, not gameplay. Original CODE 9 writes a gamma-table pointer through guest address zero before Control; Systemless rejects that record with paramErr. [#4688](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4688) tracks this reproduced mismatch; draft fix [#4690](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4690) passed six focused gamma tests before formatting. Fresh committed native `c71c24bb336339225daa16babb6e5614714ef6b6` reads the real table `$00208280` through guest address zero and returns success. The bounded New Game replay still has an inspected black screen, so the parameter repair is not a gameplay qualification; CI exposed a formatter filename accidentally retained in the test file, locally corrected with final tests pending. Bounded gameplay, browser validation, content-only screenshot, normal promotion/CI/merge, human release and public replay remain pending. No catalogue entry or hosted game asset yet.

### Brick Attack: measured ball flight and input follow-up

The apparent New Game exit above is superseded by a normal menu interaction: move the pointer onto New Game, allow 30 frontend ticks for the original menu to update, then click. This starts the first level (94 bricks), without a game patch or emulator change. On the same original archive/native `9a8e9a62`, mouse launch produces visible ball flight and a red brick disappears. Fresh replay passes **11 measured pixel assertions**, **1,725 frontend/guest ticks**, zero exhausted frames. Captures were inspected before deriving assertions; this is bounded collision evidence, not a complete game or browser claim.

Right-arrow during active flight and mouse movement both leave the paddle at the left edge. A separate key-delivery run confirms the right-arrow bit in guest KeyMap (`$0182 = $0010`), one assertion passing at 1,745 ticks; this proves input delivery, not paddle response. Paddle control, score behaviour, original bundled-library distribution scope and browser/hosting gates remain open in [#4689](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4689).

Full original `Airburst Manual.html` credits Aaron and Adam Fothergill, copyright 2001, explicitly permits free complete distribution with the manual and every associated file unchanged, and prohibits redistributing personalised registration keys. Original native startup initially halts at PC 0 inside RezLib. Rebuilt source `fc21c6f13a38f8815a1335731c13858b3d48d9b8` advances the untouched package beyond RezLib initialization, then stops at unsupported `Apple;Carbon;Multimedia:CancelCallBack`, PC `01F003F0`, LR `01011B18`, 5,750 cycles. [QuickTime callback lifecycle #4705](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4705) remains open; a nil-only success stub does not qualify the callback family.

Further untouched-original replay (13 October 2026): with the in-progress callback lifecycle and merged Carbon movie/QuickDraw fixes, startup advances to `BeginFullScreen`. [Owned fullscreen PR #4742](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4742) passes seventeen fullscreen-related tests and all 104 PPC Window Manager regressions. The combined native build displays the inspected original logo/loading screen for thirty frontend/guest ticks, with zero exhaustion; this startup script has no gameplay assertions. Read-only first-frame debugger inspection confirms actual restore state `0301FFD0`, colour window `0301FFE0`, live PixMap handle `030200D0`, and rectangle `(0,0,600,800)`. It does not verify transient dimensions after the caller reused its stack.

A longer bounded replay halts at frontend tick 55 / guest tick 54 on `CarbonLib:sin`, PC `01F00184`, SP `07F6FCC0`, LR `01016004`, zero exhausted frames. [Sine binding #4741](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4741) tracks that next defect. The combined build includes unmerged callback work; it is evidence of progress, not a released-runtime compatibility claim.

Merged fullscreen [#4742](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4742), observed Carbon math exports [#4744](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4744), and genuine owned cooperative callback lifecycle [#4746](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4746) now resolve those startup/game-entry blockers. All 85 relevant callback/movie/UPP/controller tests pass, including actual guest argument/TOC delivery, strict SDK time boundaries, signed-short type flags and disposal isolation. All 39 math tests and 104 Window Manager tests pass.

Fresh clean merged master `a8fe92e087f4ce5838cf9e8da9988fa6818d5925`, original archive, exact PPC selector and depth 16: normal menu/Play starts the four-player arena. BEM's default held left/right arrows rotate its bat; a live ball and CPU score progression pass **25 fixed measured pixel checks** at **722 frontend / 722 guest ticks**, zero exhaustion. The matched no-key run passes **nine stationary-bat checks** at the same elapsed ticks. Fresh captures are inspected and the full guest arena screenshot contains no desktop, menu bar, emulator framing or host UI. No human-player scoring, complete-game, saving or audio fidelity claim is made. Original Options background artifacts are tracked in [#4745](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/issues/4745).

[Catalogue submission #4748](https://github.com/benletchford/systemless/pull/4748) stages that real screenshot and the exact intact original archive with the unchanged-package/manual/key restrictions. Normal CI asset upload and source rewriting completed at `aee3719a076ef02bc5235ec753c1677f2d2c1569`; its final website-check dispatch hit GitHub HTTP 500 after the commit. Fresh managed archive and screenshot downloads independently match 7,760,231 bytes / original SHA-256 and 703,170 bytes / `1efb53cb6f57752dc82a4cff78d3c7173d813f18cff533fbe28da423be520904`. The temporary original-byte transfer is retired. Normal menu/Play, live ball and CPU score progression are inspected. Pacing is **61.017 / 62.172 guest ticks/sec**, with no console errors and the unchanged minimum 50 satisfied. The unreleased frontend uses a production-origin test bridge; actual managed archive requests are untouched, no local archive fixture or browser security override is used. Compositor captures show the complete arena; raw exports can catch a different intermediate image, so no exact frame-oracle equivalence is claimed. Browser launch is approved in #4748. ### Spacestation Pheta direct public replay — qualified bounded gameplay
